psutil\n+\n+                proc = psutil.Process(pid)\n+                if abs(proc.create_time() - start_time) > 0.1:\n+                    return False\n+            except Exception:\n+ guardian_main(worker_id: str, worker_pid: int, log_queue, config: dict[str, str]):\n+    worker_start_time = None\n+    try:\n+        import psutil\n+\n+        worker_start_time =      video = np.stack(frames, axis=0).astype(np.float32)\n+        video = (video / 127.5) - 1.0\n+        video = torch.from_numpy(video).permute(3, 0, 1, 2)\n+\n+        print(f\"[UNI3C] any2video: Guide video tensor shape: {tuple(video.shape)}, dtype: {video.dtype}\")\n+        print(f\"[UNI3C] any2video:   value range: [{video.min().item():.2f}, {video.max().item():.2f}]\")\n+        return video\n+\n+    def _apply_uni3c_frame_policy(\n+        self,\n+        frames: list,\n+        target_frames: int,\n+        policy: str,\n+    ) -> list:\n+        current = len(frames)\n+\n+        if policy == \"off\":\n+            if current != target_frames:\n+                raise ValueError(\n+                    f\"[UNI3C] Frame count mismatch: guide has {current} frames, \"\n+                    f\"target is {target_frames}. Use a different frame_policy.\"\n+                )\n+            return frames\n+\n+        if policy == \"fit\":\n+            if current == target_frames:\n+                return frames\n+            indices = np.linspace(0, current - 1, target_frames).astype(int)\n+            return [frames[i] for i in indices]\n+\n+        if policy == \"trim\":\n+            if current >= target_frames:\n+                return frames[:target_frames]\n+            return frames + [frames[-1]] * (target_frames - current)\n+\n+        if policy == \"loop\":\n+            if current >= target_frames:\n+                return frames[:target_frames]\n+            result = []\n+            while len(result) < target_frames:\n+                result.extend(frames)\n+            return result[:target_frames]\n+\n+        raise ValueError(f\"[UNI3C] Unknown frame_policy: {policy}\")\n+\n+    def _detect_empty_frames(\n+        self,\n+        guide_video: torch.Tensor,\n+        threshold: float = 0.02,\n+    ) -> list:\n+        with torch.no_grad():\n+            delta_from_black = (guide_video + 1.0).abs()\n+            per_frame_delta = delta_from_black.mean(dim=(0, 2, 3))\n+            empty = per_frame_delta < threshold\n+        return [bool(x) for x in empty.tolist()]\n+\n+    def _map_pixel_frames_to_latent_frames(\n+        self,\n+        num_pixel_frames: int,\n+        num_latent_frames: int,\n+    ) -> list:\n+        mapping = []\n+        for lat_f in range(num_latent_frames):\n+            start_pix = lat_f * 4\n+            end_pix = min(start_pix + 4, num_pixel_frames)\n+            mapping.append(list(range(start_pix, end_pix)))\n+        return mapping\n+\n+    def _encode_uni3c_guide(\n+        self,\n+        guide_video: torch.Tensor,\n+        VAE_tile_size: int,\n+        expected_channels: int = 20,\n+        zero_empty_frames: bool = True,\n+    ) -> torch.Tensor:\n+        num_pixel_frames = guide_video.shape[1]\n+        empty_pixel_mask = []\n+        if zero_empty_frames:\n+            empty_pixel_mask = self._detect_empty_frames(guide_video)\n+            num_empty = sum(empty_pixel_mask)\n+            if num_empty > 0:\n+                print(f\"[UNI3C] any2video: Detected {num_empty}/{num_pixel_frames} empty pixel frames\")\n+                ranges = []\n+                start = None\n+                for i, is_empty in enumerate(empty_pixel_mask):\n+                    if is_empty and start is None:\n+                        start = i\n+                    elif not is_empty and start is not None:\n+                        ranges.append(f\"{start}-{i-1}\" if i - 1 > start else str(start))\n+                        start = None\n+                if start is not None:\n+                    ranges.append(f\"{start}-{num_pixel_frames-1}\" if num_pixel_frames - 1 > start else str(start))\n+                if ranges:\n+                    print(f\"[UNI3C] any2video:   Empty frame ranges: {', '.join(ranges)}\")\n+\n+        guide_video = guide_video.to(device=self.device, dtype=self.VAE_dtype)\n+        latent = self.vae.encode([guide_video], tile_size=VAE_tile_size)[0]\n+        render_latent = latent.unsqueeze(0)\n+\n+        print(f\"[UNI3C] any2video: VAE encoded render_latent shape: {tuple(render_latent.shape)}\")\n+        print(f\"[UNI3C] any2video:   Expected channels: {expected_channels}, actual: {render_latent.shape[1]}\")\n+        print(\n+            f\"[UNI3C_DIAG] Latent stats: mean={render_latent.mean().item():.4f}, \"\n+            f\"std={render_latent.std().item():.4f}\"\n+        )\n+        print(\n+            f\"[UNI3C_DIAG] Latent range: min={render_latent.min().item():.4f}, \"\n+            f\"max={render_latent.max().item():.4f}\"\n+        )\n+\n+        if zero_empty_frames and any(empty_pixel_mask):\n+            num_latent_frames = render_latent.shape[2]\n+            pixel_to_latent = self._map_pixel_frames_to_latent_frames(num_pixel_frames, num_latent_frames)\n+            zeroed_count = 0\n+            for lat_f, pixel_indices in enumerate(pixel_to_latent):\n+                if all(empty_pixel_mask[p] for p in pixel_indices if p < len(empty_pixel_mask)):\n+                    render_latent[:, :, lat_f, :, :] = 0.0\n+                    zeroed_count += 1\n+            if zeroed_count > 0:\n+                print(f\"[UNI3C] any2video: Zeroed {zeroed_count}/{num_latent_frames} latent frames (no control signal)\")\n+\n+        if render_latent.shape[1] == 16 and expected_channels == 20:\n+            print(\"[UNI3C] any2video: Padding channels 16 -> 20\")\n+            padding = torch.zeros_like(render_latent[:, :4])\n+            render_latent = torch.cat([render_latent, padding], dim=1)\n+            print(f\"[UNI3C] any2video:   After padding: {tuple(render_latent.shape)}\")\n+\n+        return render_latent\n \n     def generate(self,\n*** End Patch\n"
}

  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/source/media/structure/composite_result.py\n+\"\"\"Result helpers for composite guidance video creation.\"\"\"\n+\n+from __future__ import annotations\n+\n+from dataclasses import dataclass\n+from pathlib import Path\n+from typing import Callable\n+\n+\n+@dataclass\n+class GuidanceCompositeResult:\n+    \"\"\"Outcome of composite-guidance-video creation.\"\"\"\n+\n+    status: str\n+    output_path: Path | None = None\n+\n+\n+def create_composite_guidance_video_result(\n+    *,\n+    create_fn: Callable[..., Path],\n+    structure_configs: list[dict],\n+    total_frames: int,\n+    structure_type: str,\n+    target_resolution: tuple[int, int],\n+    target_fps: int,\n+    output_path: Path,\n+    **kwargs,\n+) -> GuidanceCompositeResult:\n+    \"\"\"Create a result object around a composite-guidance-video builder.\"\"\"\n+    if not structure_configs:\n+        return GuidanceCompositeResult(status=\"skipped\", output_path=None)\n+\n+    created_path = create_fn(\n+        structure_configs=structure_configs,\n+        total_frames=total_frames,\n+        structure_type=structure_type,\n+        target_resolution=target_resolution,\n+        target_fps=target_fps,\n+        output_path=output_path,\n+        **kwargs,\n+    )\n+    return GuidanceCompositeResult(status=\"created\", output_path=Path(created_path))\n*** End Patch\n"
}

{
  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/source/media/video/ingest.py\n+\"\"\"Video-ingest helpers with injectable download seams for tests/runtime.\"\"\"\n+\n+from __future__ import annotations\n+\n+from pathlib import Path\n+from urllib.parse import urlparse\n+\n+import httpx\n+import requests\n+\n+from source.utils.download_utils import _get_unique_target_path\n+\n+\n+class DownloadError(RuntimeError):\n+    \"\"\"Raised when a remote video download fails.\"\"\"\n+\n+\n+def _default_stream_download(url: str, destination_path: Path, timeout: int) -> None:\n+    with requests.Session() as session:\n+        response = session.get(url, stream=True, timeout=timeout)\n+        response.raise_for_status()\n+        with open(destination_path, \"wb\") as handle:\n+            for chunk in response.iter_content(chunk_size=8192):\n+                if chunk:\n+                    handle.write(chunk)\n+\n+\n+def download_file_if_url(\n+    file_url_or_path: str,\n+    download_target_dir: Path | str | None,\n+    *,\n+    task_id_for_logging: str | None = \"generic_task\",\n+    descriptive_name: str | None = None,\n+    default_extension: str = \".mp4\",\n+    default_stem: str = \"input\",\n+    file_type_label: str = \"video\",\n+    timeout: int = 300,\n+    stream_download_impl=None,\n+) -> str:\n+    \"\"\"Download a URL to a local file, or return the original path unchanged.\"\"\"\n+    del task_id_for_logging, file_type_label  # Kept for compatibility.\n+\n+    if not file_url_or_path:\n+        return file_url_or_path\n+\n+    parsed = urlparse(file_url_or_path)\n+    if parsed.scheme not in {\"http\", \"https\"} or not download_target_dir:\n+        return file_url_or_path\n+\n+    target_dir = Path(download_target_dir)\n+    target_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n+\n+    original_name = Path(parsed.path).name\n+    extension = Path(original_name).suffix or default_extension\n+    stem = descriptive_name or Path(original_name).stem or default_stem\n+    destination_path = _get_unique_target_path(target_dir, stem[:50], extension)\n+\n+    downloader = stream_download_impl or _default_stream_download\n+    try:\n+        downloader(file_url_or_path, destination_path, timeout)\n+    except httpx.HTTPError as exc:\n+        raise DownloadError(str(exc)) from exc\n+    except OSError as exc:\n+        raise DownloadError(str(exc)) from exc\n+\n+    return str(destination_path)\n*** End Patch\n"
}
